{"etymology_number":"3","forms":[{"form":"rings","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The set of integers, #92;mathbb#123;Z#125;, is the prototypical ring.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["An algebraic structure which consists of a set with two binary operations: an additive operation and a multiplicative operation, such that the set is an abelian group under the additive operation, a monoid under the multiplicative operation, and such that the multiplicative operation is distributive with respect to the additive operation."],"topics":["algebra","mathematics","sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The definition of ring without unity allows, for instance, the set 2#92;mathbb#123;Z#125; of even integers to be a ring.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["An algebraic structure as above, but only required to be a semigroup under the multiplicative operation, that is, there need not be a multiplicative identity element."],"synonyms":[{"word":"rng"}],"topics":["algebra","mathematics","sciences"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"rĭng","tags":["General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ɹɪŋ/","tags":["General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈɹɪŋɡ/","tags":["Midlands","Northern-England"]}],"upstream_line":33998,"upstream_offset":234908810,"upstream_record_sha256":"895bd156c036fa045a3b6b0f672ee82d1d25ea8646c05c201364d0e78cf4dbcd","word":"ring"}
